## Implementation Instructions

### Step 1: Define CaptureEventProperties

Create `lib/features/camera/analytics/capture_event_properties.dart`:

These properties appear on every one of the four events. Populate them
from the objects available at the capture decision point.

```dart
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import '../sensor/pitch_level.dart';

/// Properties shared across all four capture analytics events.
@immutable
class CaptureEventProperties {
  /// Active PitchLevel at the moment of the capture attempt.
  /// Sourced from PitchBandEvaluator — the level the user was aiming for.
  final PitchLevel pitchLevel;

  /// Raw pitch angle in radians at moment of attempt.
  /// Sourced from SensorFrame.pitch of the most recent frame.
  final double pitchRad;

  /// StabilityScore.score at moment of attempt. Range [0.0, 1.0].
  final double stabilityScore;

  /// StabilityScore.confidence at moment of attempt. Range [0.0, 1.0].
  final double stabilityConfidence;

  /// SensorFrame.timestamp of the frame used for the capture decision.
  /// Microseconds since Unix epoch.
  final int sensorTimestampUs;

  /// Device model string. Sourced from device_info_plus at service init.
  final String deviceModel;

  /// Platform: 'android' or 'ios'.
  final String platform;

  const CaptureEventProperties({
    required this.pitchLevel,
    required this.pitchRad,
    required this.stabilityScore,
    required this.stabilityConfidence,
    required this.sensorTimestampUs,
    required this.deviceModel,
    required this.platform,
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() => {
        'pitch_level':           pitchLevel.name,
        'pitch_rad':             pitchRad,
        'stability_score':       stabilityScore,
        'stability_confidence':  stabilityConfidence,
        'sensor_timestamp_us':   sensorTimestampUs,
        'device_model':          deviceModel,
        'platform':              platform,
      };
}
```

### Step 2: Define the CaptureAnalyticsEvent sealed hierarchy

Create `lib/features/camera/analytics/capture_analytics_event.dart`:

```dart
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'capture_event_properties.dart';

/// Sealed base for all four capture pipeline analytics events.
/// Each subtype maps to exactly one analytics event name.
sealed class CaptureAnalyticsEvent {
  final CaptureEventProperties properties;
  const CaptureAnalyticsEvent(this.properties);

  /// The snake_case event name sent to the analytics sink.
  String get eventName;

  /// Full property map merged from shared + event-specific properties.
  Map<String, dynamic> toMap();
}

// ─── photo_captured ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/// Fired when a capture attempt succeeds: frame is sharp, exposure is
/// acceptable, stability gate passes, and the JPEG is written to disk.
@immutable
class PhotoCapturedEvent extends CaptureAnalyticsEvent {
  /// FrameQualityResult.blur.score of the accepted frame. Higher = sharper.
  final double blurScore;

  /// FrameQualityResult.exposure.meanLuminance of the accepted frame.
  final double meanLuminance;

  /// Absolute file path of the written JPEG.
  final String framePath;

  const PhotoCapturedEvent({
    required super.properties,
    required this.blurScore,
    required this.meanLuminance,
    required this.framePath,
  });

  @override
  String get eventName => 'photo_captured';

  @override
  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() => {
        ...properties.toMap(),
        'blur_score':      blurScore,
        'mean_luminance':  meanLuminance,
        'frame_path':      framePath,
      };
}

// ─── photo_rejected_blur ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/// Fired when a capture attempt is rejected because the frame is blurry.
/// Mutually exclusive with photo_captured for the same attempt.
@immutable
class PhotoRejectedBlurEvent extends CaptureAnalyticsEvent {
  /// FrameQualityResult.blur.score that triggered rejection.
  final double blurScore;

  /// Native blur threshold at time of rejection (from native constants).
  /// Pass this in from the call site — do not hardcode here.
  final double blurThreshold;

  const PhotoRejectedBlurEvent({
    required super.properties,
    required this.blurScore,
    required this.blurThreshold,
  });

  @override
  String get eventName => 'photo_rejected_blur';

  @override
  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() => {
        ...properties.toMap(),
        'blur_score':      blurScore,
        'blur_threshold':  blurThreshold,
      };
}

// ─── photo_rejected_motion ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

/// Fired when a capture attempt is rejected because the stability score
/// is below the rejection threshold at shutter-fire time.
/// Mutually exclusive with photo_captured for the same attempt.
@immutable
class PhotoRejectedMotionEvent extends CaptureAnalyticsEvent {
  /// StabilityScore.score that triggered rejection. Always < rejection threshold.
  final double stabilityScoreAtRejection;

  /// The threshold the score failed to meet.
  final double rejectionThreshold;

  /// gyroMag of the most recent SensorFrame — quantifies how much motion.
  final double gyroMag;

  const PhotoRejectedMotionEvent({
    required super.properties,
    required this.stabilityScoreAtRejection,
    required this.rejectionThreshold,
    required this.gyroMag,
  });

  @override
  String get eventName => 'photo_rejected_motion';

  @override
  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() => {
        ...properties.toMap(),
        'stability_score_at_rejection': stabilityScoreAtRejection,
        'rejection_threshold':          rejectionThreshold,
        'gyro_mag':                     gyroMag,
      };
}

// ─── photo_warned_exposure ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

/// Fired when the exposure is outside acceptable range at the moment
/// the user attempts a capture. This event fires INDEPENDENTLY of
/// capture outcome — it may co-occur with photo_captured (capture
/// succeeded despite marginal exposure) or with a rejection event
/// (if blur or motion also triggered). Never suppressed.
@immutable
class PhotoWarnedExposureEvent extends CaptureAnalyticsEvent {
  /// true if ExposureResult.isUnderexposed was true.
  final bool isUnderexposed;

  /// true if ExposureResult.isOverexposed was true.
  final bool isOverexposed;

  /// ExposureResult.meanLuminance at the time of the warning.
  final double meanLuminance;

  const PhotoWarnedExposureEvent({
    required super.properties,
    required this.isUnderexposed,
    required this.isOverexposed,
    required this.meanLuminance,
  });

  @override
  String get eventName => 'photo_warned_exposure';

  @override
  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() => {
        ...properties.toMap(),
        'is_underexposed': isUnderexposed,
        'is_overexposed':  isOverexposed,
        'mean_luminance':  meanLuminance,
      };
}
```

### Step 3: Define the analytics sink interface

Create `lib/features/camera/analytics/analytics_sink.dart`:

If an existing analytics service was found in "Files to Inspect First",
adapt this interface to match its method signature exactly — do not create
a second dispatch mechanism.

If no analytics service exists, define:

```dart
/// Minimal interface for dispatching named events with properties.
/// Implement against the real analytics backend (Mixpanel, Amplitude,
/// custom endpoint, etc.) in a separate task.
abstract interface class AnalyticsSink {
  Future<void> track(String eventName, Map<String, dynamic> properties);
}
```

This interface is the only seam unit tests need — they inject a
`_FakeAnalyticsSink` that records calls.

### Step 4: Implement CaptureAnalyticsService

Create `lib/features/camera/analytics/capture_analytics_service.dart`:

```dart
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'analytics_sink.dart';
import 'capture_analytics_event.dart';
import 'capture_event_properties.dart';

/// Builds and dispatches capture pipeline analytics events.
///
/// Inject [AnalyticsSink] at construction time.
/// Call [dispatch] once per capture decision — never call it twice
/// for the same attempt.
class CaptureAnalyticsService {
  final AnalyticsSink _sink;

  const CaptureAnalyticsService(this._sink);

  /// Dispatches [event] to the analytics sink.
  /// Logs a debug warning if called with a null-equivalent event in
  /// debug mode but never throws — analytics must never crash the pipeline.
  Future<void> dispatch(CaptureAnalyticsEvent event) async {
    try {
      await _sink.track(event.eventName, event.toMap());
    } catch (e, stack) {
      // Analytics dispatch failure must NEVER propagate to the capture
      // pipeline. Log only — do not rethrow.
      debugPrint('[CaptureAnalytics] dispatch failed: $e\n$stack');
    }
  }
}
```

### Step 5: Wire events into the capture decision call sites

In `lib/features/camera/capture_controller.dart`, add dispatch calls at
the following three decision points. Read the file to identify exact line
locations before editing. Do NOT restructure the capture logic — add
dispatch calls only.

**Wiring point 1 — Capture accepted (after JPEG is written)**

After the line where `capturedFrameProvider` is set with a non-null
`CapturedFrame`, add:

```dart
await _analyticsService.dispatch(PhotoCapturedEvent(
  properties: _buildProperties(latestSensorFrame, activePitchLevel),
  blurScore:   frameQualityResult.blur.score,
  meanLuminance: frameQualityResult.exposure.meanLuminance,
  framePath:   capturedFrame.path, // or bytes path — adjust to actual field
));
```

**Wiring point 2 — Capture rejected due to blur**

At the point where `frameQualityResult.blur.isBlurry == true` causes
the capture to be suppressed (find the guard condition), add:

```dart
await _analyticsService.dispatch(PhotoRejectedBlurEvent(
  properties:    _buildProperties(latestSensorFrame, activePitchLevel),
  blurScore:     frameQualityResult.blur.score,
  blurThreshold: kNativeBlurThreshold, // read from the threshold constant
                                        // identified in the blur QA task;
                                        // if not a Dart constant yet,
                                        // use 0.0 and file a follow-up task
));
```

**Wiring point 3 — Capture rejected due to motion**

At the point where `stabilityScore.score < kStabilityRejectionThreshold`
causes suppression, add:

```dart
await _analyticsService.dispatch(PhotoRejectedMotionEvent(
  properties:                _buildProperties(latestSensorFrame, activePitchLevel),
  stabilityScoreAtRejection: stabilityScore.score,
  rejectionThreshold:        kStabilityRejectionThreshold,
  gyroMag:                   latestSensorFrame.gyroMag,
));
```

**Wiring point 4 — Exposure warning (independent of capture outcome)**

At the point where `FrameQualityResult` is available and
`exposure.isUnderexposed || exposure.isOverexposed` is true, add
BEFORE the capture/rejection decision so the warning fires regardless
of outcome:

```dart
if (frameQualityResult.exposure.isUnderexposed ||
    frameQualityResult.exposure.isOverexposed) {
  await _analyticsService.dispatch(PhotoWarnedExposureEvent(
    properties:    _buildProperties(latestSensorFrame, activePitchLevel),
    isUnderexposed: frameQualityResult.exposure.isUnderexposed,
    isOverexposed:  frameQualityResult.exposure.isOverexposed,
    meanLuminance:  frameQualityResult.exposure.meanLuminance,
  ));
}
```

**Add the _buildProperties helper to CaptureController:**

```dart
CaptureEventProperties _buildProperties(
  SensorFrame frame,
  PitchLevel activePitchLevel,
) =>
    CaptureEventProperties(
      pitchLevel:           activePitchLevel,
      pitchRad:             frame.pitch,
      stabilityScore:       _lastStabilityScore?.score ?? 0.0,
      stabilityConfidence:  _lastStabilityScore?.confidence ?? 0.0,
      sensorTimestampUs:    frame.timestamp,
      deviceModel:          _deviceModel,   // populated at controller init
      platform:             Platform.isIOS ? 'ios' : 'android',
    );
```

**Inject CaptureAnalyticsService into CaptureController:**

Add `final CaptureAnalyticsService _analyticsService` as a constructor
parameter with a default:

```dart
CaptureController({
  CaptureAnalyticsService? analyticsService,
  // ... existing params
}) : _analyticsService = analyticsService ??
         CaptureAnalyticsService(_ProductionAnalyticsSink());
```

If no production sink exists yet, use a no-op implementation:

```dart
class _NoOpAnalyticsSink implements AnalyticsSink {
  const _NoOpAnalyticsSink();
  @override
  Future<void> track(String eventName, Map<String, dynamic> properties)
      async {} // intentionally silent until real sink is wired
}
```

### Step 6: Write unit tests

Create `test/features/camera/analytics/capture_analytics_test.dart`.
Use `package:test` only.

Define a call-recording fake at the top:

```dart
class _FakeAnalyticsSink implements AnalyticsSink {
  final List<({String eventName, Map<String, dynamic> properties})> calls = [];

  @override
  Future<void> track(String eventName, Map<String, dynamic> properties)
      async => calls.add((eventName: eventName, properties: properties));

  void reset() => calls.clear();
}
```

Define a base properties fixture:

```dart
const kTestProperties = CaptureEventProperties(
  pitchLevel:          PitchLevel.eye,
  pitchRad:            0.0,
  stabilityScore:      0.85,
  stabilityConfidence: 1.0,
  sensorTimestampUs:   1000000,
  deviceModel:         'Test Device',
  platform:            'android',
);
```

Implement ALL of the following test cases:

**group: 'CaptureEventProperties.toMap()'**

1. **toMap() includes all 7 required keys**
   Assert the returned map contains exactly the keys:
   `pitch_level`, `pitch_rad`, `stability_score`, `stability_confidence`,
   `sensor_timestamp_us`, `device_model`, `platform`.

2. **pitch_level serialises to PitchLevel.name string**
   Assert `toMap()['pitch_level'] == 'eye'` for `PitchLevel.eye`.

3. **All PitchLevel values serialise without throwing**
   For each of `overhead`, `eye`, `table`, `ground`:
   Assert `CaptureEventProperties(..., pitchLevel: level).toMap()`
   returns `'pitch_level': level.name`.

**group: 'PhotoCapturedEvent'**

4. **eventName == 'photo_captured'**

5. **toMap() contains all shared properties plus blur_score,
   mean_luminance, frame_path**
   Assert map contains all 7 shared keys plus the 3 event-specific keys.

6. **blur_score and mean_luminance values are preserved exactly**
   Assert `toMap()['blur_score'] == 42.5` when constructed with `blurScore: 42.5`.

**group: 'PhotoRejectedBlurEvent'**

7. **eventName == 'photo_rejected_blur'**

8. **toMap() contains blur_score and blur_threshold**
   Assert both event-specific keys present alongside all shared keys.

9. **blur_threshold value preserved**
   Assert `toMap()['blur_threshold'] == 15.0`.

**group: 'PhotoRejectedMotionEvent'**

10. **eventName == 'photo_rejected_motion'**

11. **toMap() contains stability_score_at_rejection, rejection_threshold,
    gyro_mag**

12. **stability_score_at_rejection is always < rejection_threshold**
    Construct with `stabilityScoreAtRejection: 0.3, rejectionThreshold: 0.4`.
    Assert `toMap()['stability_score_at_rejection'] < toMap()['rejection_threshold']`.

**group: 'PhotoWarnedExposureEvent'**

13. **eventName == 'photo_warned_exposure'**

14. **toMap() contains is_underexposed, is_overexposed, mean_luminance**

15. **is_underexposed: true, is_overexposed: false serialises correctly**
    Assert `toMap()['is_underexposed'] == true`,
    `toMap()['is_overexposed'] == false`.

16. **is_overexposed: true, is_underexposed: false serialises correctly**

**group: 'CaptureAnalyticsService.dispatch()'**

17. **dispatch(PhotoCapturedEvent) calls sink.track with 'photo_captured'**
    Assert `fake.calls.last.eventName == 'photo_captured'`.

18. **dispatch(PhotoRejectedBlurEvent) calls sink with 'photo_rejected_blur'**

19. **dispatch(PhotoRejectedMotionEvent) calls sink with 'photo_rejected_motion'**

20. **dispatch(PhotoWarnedExposureEvent) calls sink with 'photo_warned_exposure'**

21. **sink failure does not throw from dispatch()**
    Use a fake sink that throws `Exception('sink down')`.
    Assert `dispatch(event)` completes without throwing.

22. **dispatch() is called exactly once per capture decision**
    Simulate a successful capture decision: call dispatch once with
    `PhotoCapturedEvent`. Assert `fake.calls.length == 1`.

23. **photo_captured and photo_rejected_blur never fire for same attempt**
    This is an architectural constraint — verify by asserting that calling
    dispatch twice with `PhotoCapturedEvent` and `PhotoRejectedBlurEvent`
    in the same attempt is not done by the service (the service has no
    guard; the guard is at the call site). Write this as a documentation
    test: assert `fake.calls.length == 2` when dispatch is called twice,
    then add a comment: `// INVARIANT: call sites must never dispatch
    // both photo_captured and a rejection event for the same attempt.
    // This test confirms the service does not enforce it — the controller
    // logic does.`

24. **exposure warning fires independently of capture outcome**
    Dispatch `PhotoWarnedExposureEvent` followed by `PhotoCapturedEvent`.
    Assert `fake.calls.length == 2`.
    Assert `fake.calls[0].eventName == 'photo_warned_exposure'`.
    Assert `fake.calls[1].eventName == 'photo_captured'`.

25. **All shared property keys appear in every event's toMap()**
    For all four event types, assert each of the 7 shared keys is present
    in `toMap()`.

**group: 'CaptureEventProperties — property values'**

26. **platform field is 'android' or 'ios' — no other values accepted**
    Construct with `platform: 'windows'`. There is no runtime validation
    (it is a string field) — assert `toMap()['platform'] == 'windows'` and
    add a `// NOTE:` comment: platform validation is the responsibility of
    the call site, not the value type.

27. **stabilityScore outside [0.0, 1.0] is stored as-is (no clamping)**
    Construct with `stabilityScore: 1.5`. Assert `toMap()['stability_score'] == 1.5`.
    Add a `// NOTE:` comment: clamping is StabilityScorer's responsibility.

---

## Analytics Events

**Event: `photo_captured`**
Trigger: Capture attempt succeeds — frame is sharp, exposure acceptable,
         stability gate passes, JPEG written to disk
Properties (all shared + event-specific):
  - pitch_level: string (PitchLevel.name)
  - pitch_rad: double
  - stability_score: double
  - stability_confidence: double
  - sensor_timestamp_us: int
  - device_model: string
  - platform: 'android' | 'ios'
  - blur_score: double
  - mean_luminance: double
  - frame_path: string

**Event: `photo_rejected_blur`**
Trigger: Capture attempt rejected — FrameQualityResult.blur.isBlurry == true
Properties (all shared + event-specific):
  - [all 7 shared]
  - blur_score: double
  - blur_threshold: double

**Event: `photo_rejected_motion`**
Trigger: Capture attempt rejected — stabilityScore.score < kStabilityRejectionThreshold
Properties (all shared + event-specific):
  - [all 7 shared]
  - stability_score_at_rejection: double
  - rejection_threshold: double
  - gyro_mag: double

**Event: `photo_warned_exposure`**
Trigger: ExposureResult.isUnderexposed || isOverexposed at capture attempt time.
         Fires before and independently of capture/rejection decision.
Properties (all shared + event-specific):
  - [all 7 shared]
  - is_underexposed: bool
  - is_overexposed: bool
  - mean_luminance: double

**Mutual exclusivity rule:**
For a single capture attempt, exactly one of `photo_captured`,
`photo_rejected_blur`, or `photo_rejected_motion` must fire.
`photo_warned_exposure` may fire in addition to any of the above.
No other combination is valid.

---

## What NOT to Change

- Do NOT modify `sensor_frame.dart`, `stability_scorer.dart`,
  `frame_quality_result.dart`, `blur_result.dart`,
  `exposure_result.dart`, or `pitch_band_evaluator.dart`
- Do NOT modify the capture logic in `capture_controller.dart` beyond
  adding the dispatch calls at the four wiring points — do not reorder
  conditions, add new guards, or change return types
- Do NOT create a Riverpod provider for `CaptureAnalyticsService` in
  this task — it is injected directly into `CaptureController` via
  the constructor; provider wiring is a separate task
- Do NOT implement a real analytics backend (Mixpanel, Amplitude, etc.)
  in this task — the `_NoOpAnalyticsSink` placeholder is correct until
  the backend task runs
- Do NOT add `photo_warned_exposure` to the mutual exclusivity guard —
  it deliberately fires independently; adding it to the guard would
  suppress legitimate warnings
- Do NOT use `mockito` or `mocktail` — `_FakeAnalyticsSink` is a
  hand-written local class per project convention

---

## Edge Cases to Handle

- [ ] `CaptureAnalyticsService.dispatch()` sink throws → caught internally,
      logged via `debugPrint`, never rethrown; unit test #21 covers this;
      a broken analytics connection must never prevent a capture from
      completing
- [ ] Both `isUnderexposed` and `isOverexposed` are true simultaneously
      (hardware fault) → `PhotoWarnedExposureEvent` still fires with both
      set to true; the `ExposureResult` assert in debug mode will have
      already flagged this upstream; analytics must not add a second guard
- [ ] `kNativeBlurThreshold` is not yet a named Dart constant when this
      task runs → pass `0.0` as the threshold in the wiring; add a
      `// TODO: replace with real threshold constant` comment; file
      BUG-QA-ANALYTICS-THRESHOLD in the report section of the commit
      message
- [ ] `_buildProperties` called before `_deviceModel` is populated
      (controller init race) → default `_deviceModel` to `'unknown'`
      if not yet populated; never block capture on device info fetch
- [ ] `activePitchLevel` is null at wiring point (no level selected yet)
      → default to `PitchLevel.eye` if null; document this default with
      a `// DEFAULT:` comment at the call site
- [ ] Dispatch called more than once for the same capture attempt due to
      a double-trigger bug upstream → `CaptureAnalyticsService` does not
      deduplicate; the call site is responsible; if observed in testing,
      file a bug against `CaptureController`, not the analytics service

---

## Constraints

- `CaptureAnalyticsService.dispatch()` must be `async` and must
  `await _sink.track()` — fire-and-forget is not acceptable; the caller
  awaits dispatch so that test assertions on `fake.calls` are synchronous
- `CaptureAnalyticsEvent` must be a `sealed` class — this enforces
  exhaustive pattern matching in any future switch on event type and
  prevents undocumented subtypes being added without updating this file
- All four `toMap()` implementations must spread `properties.toMap()`
  first and then add event-specific keys — event-specific keys must
  never shadow shared keys; if a key collision is introduced in future,
  the event-specific key wins silently; add a `// NOTE:` comment to
  this effect in the base `toMap()` pattern
- `CaptureEventProperties` must be `const`-constructible — confirmed by
  using `const kTestProperties` in the test file
- The `AnalyticsSink` interface file must be the only place the sink
  contract is defined — do not duplicate the `track()` signature
  elsewhere

---

## Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] All 27 unit tests in `capture_analytics_test.dart` pass with
      `flutter test`
- [ ] All pre-existing tests in `test/features/camera/` and
      `test/qa/` still pass — zero regressions
- [ ] `flutter analyze lib/features/camera/analytics/` — zero errors,
      zero warnings
- [ ] `CaptureAnalyticsEvent` is `sealed` — confirmed by attempting to
      subclass it outside its file and verifying a compile error
- [ ] `const kTestProperties = CaptureEventProperties(...)` compiles
      without error — confirms `const`-constructibility
- [ ] `PhotoCapturedEvent.toMap()` contains all 10 expected keys
      (7 shared + 3 event-specific) — verified by unit test #5
- [ ] `CaptureAnalyticsService.dispatch()` with a throwing sink does not
      throw — verified by unit test #21
- [ ] `photo_warned_exposure` and `photo_captured` can both appear in
      `fake.calls` for the same capture attempt — verified by unit test #24
- [ ] No `print()` calls in any new file — `debugPrint` only, wrapped in
      the existing catch block
- [ ] `flutter test --coverage` reports ≥ 90% line coverage across all
      files in `lib/features/camera/analytics/`

## Testing Instructions

1. Run unit tests:
flutter test test/features/camera/analytics/capture_analytics_test.dart
2. Run the full camera and sensor test suite for regression:
flutter test test/features/camera/

flutter test test/qa/
3. Run `flutter analyze lib/features/camera/analytics/` — zero issues.
4. Attempt to add a fifth subclass of `CaptureAnalyticsEvent` in a
   scratch file outside the sealed class file; confirm the analyser
   rejects it. Remove before committing.
5. Run `flutter test --coverage`; confirm ≥ 90% line coverage on all
   five new files in `lib/features/camera/analytics/` via
   `coverage/lcov.info`.
6. On a physical device (optional but recommended): run a debug build,
   trigger captures in each outcome state (success, blur rejection,
   motion rejection, exposure warning), and confirm `debugPrint`
   output appears in the console for each event name. This verifies
   the wiring points in `CaptureController` are correct.

## Assumptions

- Assumed: no production analytics sink exists yet — `_NoOpAnalyticsSink`
  is used as the default; if a real sink already exists, pass it as
  the default in `CaptureController`'s constructor and remove
  `_NoOpAnalyticsSink`; do not break the existing sink's call contract
- Assumed: `kStabilityRejectionThreshold` is defined as a Dart constant
  accessible from `CaptureController` at the time of wiring — if it is
  not yet a named constant (it was a test-local constant in the QA tasks),
  define it in `stability_scorer.dart` as
  `const double kStabilityRejectionThreshold = 0.4;` before wiring;
  this is a one-line production change that is acceptable within scope
- Assumed: `CaptureController` has access to the `activePitchLevel` and
  `latestSensorFrame` at the capture decision point — if these are not
  currently in scope at the wiring location, read them from the nearest
  available provider or state field; do not restructure the controller
  to accommodate them in this task
- Assumed: `device_info_plus` is already in `pubspec.yaml` (confirmed
  in the folder lifecycle QA task); `_deviceModel` is populated once at
  `CaptureController` init via `DeviceInfoPlugin` and cached as a
  `String` field
